问题与改进

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39502963850 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 2

重要指引错误上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · 认证 Skill 漏掉 preparation，应用只能照不完整的客户端契约处理实时失败

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

Skill 第 48 行把 useAuthentication() 返回写成 {client,session,isPending,refresh}，client-session 参考 17-30 行也只讲 session/isPending/refresh；实际类型和实现还公开 preparation/retryPreparation，README 36-40 行记录十秒超时及 failed 后 retry/sign-out。应用在 verify-2 确实把 failed 当致命门禁（登录 200、authenticated=true 却停在 Unable to connect），补丁后才改为 warning 并继续挂载。因此“Skill/参考漏掉 preparation 与实时降级语义”有源码和原始事件支持。需限定：socket 失败本身还涉及应用把 realtimeURL 从 api.baseURL 派生，不能全部归因于文档；README 已文档化 preparation，缺口在 Skill/参考的入门指引。

  • 职责与公开约定：认证 Skill 的 Public surfaces 明确把 useAuthentication() 写成只返回 client/session/isPending/refresh；client-session 参考同样只解释这些字段，未提 preparation/retryPreparation，而 README 另有十秒准备超时和 failed 状态，冻结指引内部不一致。 E27 E28 E12 E66
  • 实际行为证据：auth-provider 实际用十秒计时器把未完成的实时准备 publish 为 failed，并把 preparation/retryPreparation 放进 context；这是公开运行行为，不是仅文档声明。 E13 E66
  • 应用接入与业务边界：verify-2 报告显示管理员登录成功且 authenticated=true，但进入业务界面即停在 Unable to connect，B01-B05 未执行；补丁显示应用原先把非 ready 渲染成 retry/sign-out 致命屏，后改为 failed 显示 warning 并继续挂载。 E15 E17 E14
  • 环境因素：retro 记录原故障还包含 realtimeURL 从 api.baseURL 派生导致 Origin 不匹配 403，以及代理阻断 WebSocket；这解释了触发条件，但不能解释 Skill/参考为何漏列 preparation，因此环境因素不推翻文档缺口。 E67 E13
  • 工厂因素：补丁修改的是应用自有 .agents/rules/shell.md 和 session-preparation.tsx，致命门禁来自应用规则与实现；现有材料未发现工厂提示或注入配置直接要求该门禁，工厂因素保留未知，但不需要它来解释 Skill 缺项。 E17 E14
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：插件已公开 preparation/retryPreparation，README 也文档化其超时与 failed；当前应用已能用 failed+warning 降级。能力存在，缺的是 Skill/参考的入门说明，故落在 guidance-gap 而非 capability-gap。 E12 E14 E66

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

认证 Skill 的 Public surfaces 明确写 useAuthentication() 返回 client、session、isPending、refresh，client-session 参考也只描述这些字段；但实现实际还返回 preparation 和 retryPreparation，README 另有一段十秒准备超时与 retry/sign-out。Agent 使用了 preparation 并把 failed 实现成致命门禁，Skill 和参考都没有说明实时传输降级时是否应阻断 HTTP 会话。verify-2 因此登录成功后始终停在 Unable to connect，B01-B05 全部未执行。

评审时 · 文档 / API 声称

Skill 第 48 行称 useAuthentication() 返回 `{ client, session, isPending, refresh }`；client-session 参考只解释这些字段，并称 provider 不轮询、不监听撤销。

评审时 · 实际观察

auth-provider.js 实际暴露 preparation/retryPreparation，并在十秒后发布 failed；README 第 38-40 行也描述该状态和模板的 retry/sign-out 行为。

实际影响
按 Skill 的主路径接入时，应用无法从文档得知 preparation 的存在或正确的降级语义；一旦 WebSocket 握手失败，已认证业务界面可能完全不可达。
建议改法
把 preparation、retryPreparation、十秒超时和传输降级策略补进 Skill 与 client-session 参考，并给出“HTTP 健康但 socket 被阻断时继续挂载并显示 warning”的模板示例；说明认证失败与实时失败的区分。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
管理员登录成功，HTTP 会话有效，但 /ws 握手未在十秒内完成或持续失败。
应有能力 / 行为
Skill/参考应公开 preparation 状态并说明传输降级不应等同于认证失败，给出非阻塞降级示例。
实际观察
Skill 漏列 preparation，参考只讲 session/isPending；应用把 failed 当致命门禁，verify-2 全应用不可达。
应用绕行与代价
应用改为 failed 时显示 warning 并继续挂载，同时配置同源 /ws。
改进后的验收标准
在阻断 WebSocket、保留 HTTP API 的环境中按 Skill 示例接入：业务路由可达并显示 warning；认证失效时仍回登录页；恢复 socket 后权限变更恢复实时生效。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 7 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:44–55E27

认证 Skill 的 Public surfaces 只声明 useAuthentication 返回 client/session/isPending/refresh，没有列出 preparation 或 retryPreparation。

Client, from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/client`:

- `authentication()` is the registration factory. It takes no options,
  contributes no routes, and mounts one React provider.
- `useAuthentication()` returns `{ client, session, isPending, refresh }`;
  `useAuthenticationClient()` returns the Better Auth client alone.
- `RequiredAuthentication`, `GuestAuthentication`, and
  `AuthenticationGuard({ mode })` gate a route subtree on the session.
- `AuthConfig` is Better Auth's client options, the type of the application's
  `client/config/auth.ts`.
- `authenticationClientToken` is the client's service token, used to stub the
  client in component tests.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md:17–30E28

Client session 参考只描述 session/isPending/refresh，并说明 provider 不轮询、不监听撤销；没有说明首次实时准备的失败状态。

- `session` is `null` while anonymous; `session.user` has `id`, `name`,
  `email`, `username`, `image`, and `emailVerified`.
- `isPending` is true until the first `getSession()` completes and during any
  `refresh()`. Render nothing or a placeholder while pending; do not treat
  pending as anonymous.
- `refresh()` re-reads the session from the server. Call it after any action
  that changes the session outside the provided hooks.
- `client` is the Better Auth client. Every Better Auth client plugin
  configured in `client/config/auth.ts` is available on it.

The provider does not poll and does not listen for revocation. A session
revoked on the server is noticed at the next `refresh()` or when an API call
returns `401`; treat that `401` as "sign in again", not as an application
error.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/README.zh-CN.md:36–40E12

认证文档说明 preparation 的 preparing/ready/failed、十秒初始化超时，以及默认模板在失败时只提供 retry 和 sign-out。

### Client session preparation

`useAuthentication()` exposes `preparation` (`preparing`, `ready`, or `failed`) and `retryPreparation()`, separately from the session query's `isPending` and `refresh()`. For every new user/session pair, including cookie startup, the provider reconnects an already-created realtime client with active subscriptions. It publishes readiness after the synchronous open notification finishes, so authorization invalidation precedes the first page permission check. No realtime client or no subscriptions means immediate readiness without creating a connection. Initialization times out after ten seconds; a late open cannot release the failed attempt. Retry creates a fresh handshake, and logout or a new session cancels the old attempt. Superseded session queries cannot restore an old session.

The Default and Examples templates place `SessionPreparation` inside `RequiredAuthentication` and outside the application layout. The whole signed-in region waits automatically, including routes that skip page authorization; guest and optional routes keep their existing behavior. Failed initialization offers retry and sign-out, including feedback when sign-out fails. Custom applications using only `RequiredAuthentication` must explicitly consume preparation if they need this first-page guarantee. Runtime reconnects and permission notifications continue to invalidate authorization and unmount protected pages and their portals; preparation does not retain hidden content or replace server authorization. A custom application that starts subscriptions only after mounting pages remains subject to that runtime revalidation.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:83–105E13

客户端 provider 用十秒计时器把未完成的实时准备发布为 failed，并把 preparation 状态暴露给应用。

                publish('ready');
            });
        });
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            cancelled = true;
            unsubscribe();
            publish('failed');
        }, PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS);
        // Even cookie startup reconnects: connected alone does not prove identity.
        realtime.reconnect();
        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
            clearTimeout(timer);
            unsubscribe();
        };
    }, [isPending, sessionKey, signedIn, services, retry]);
    const preparation = !signedIn
        ? 'ready'
        : !isPending &&
            prepared?.sessionKey === sessionKey &&
            prepared.retry === retry
            ? prepared.status
            : 'preparing';
app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:8–47E14

当前应用把 failed 准备降级为持久 warning 并继续挂载业务界面，不再让 HTTP 可用的会话停在致命屏。

/**
 * Waits for the first identity handshake, so pages mount with a permission snapshot for the signed-in session.
 *
 * A realtime connection can be unavailable while the HTTP API and the session are healthy: a proxy may block the
 * WebSocket, or the server may be briefly unable to resolve the session when the socket opens. The authorization
 * revision still guards every route and invalidates permissions on later notifications, so an unavailable socket must
 * not lock the user out of an otherwise working application. The handshake therefore falls back to the mounted
 * application with a warning, instead of a fatal screen that only offers retry or sign-out.
 */
export function SessionPreparation({
  children,
}: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement {
  const { preparation, retryPreparation } = useAuthentication();
  const toaster = useToaster();
  const { t } = useTranslation();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (preparation !== 'failed') return;
    const id = 'session-preparation-realtime-unavailable';
    toaster.show({
      id,
      type: 'warning',
      title: t('auth.realtimeUnavailable'),
      description: t('auth.realtimeUnavailableDescription'),
      duration: 0,
      action: {
        label: t('auth.retry'),
        onClick: () => {
          toaster.close(id);
          retryPreparation();
        },
      },
    });
  }, [preparation, retryPreparation, t, toaster]);

  if (preparation === 'preparing') {
    return <Loading className='min-h-svh' label={t('auth.preparingSession')} />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [重要][指引错误] 认证 Skill 漏掉 preparation，应用只能照不完整的客户端契约处理实时失败

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.10）、`packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md`、`packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

Skill 第 48 行把 useAuthentication() 返回写成 {client,session,isPending,refresh}，client-session 参考 17-30 行也只讲 session/isPending/refresh；实际类型和实现还公开 preparation/retryPreparation，README 36-40 行记录十秒超时及 failed 后 retry/sign-out。应用在 verify-2 确实把 failed 当致命门禁（登录 200、authenticated=true 却停在 Unable to connect），补丁后才改为 warning 并继续挂载。因此“Skill/参考漏掉 preparation 与实时降级语义”有源码和原始事件支持。需限定：socket 失败本身还涉及应用把 realtimeURL 从 api.baseURL 派生，不能全部归因于文档；README 已文档化 preparation，缺口在 Skill/参考的入门指引。

- 职责与公开约定：认证 Skill 的 Public surfaces 明确把 useAuthentication() 写成只返回 client/session/isPending/refresh；client-session 参考同样只解释这些字段，未提 preparation/retryPreparation，而 README 另有十秒准备超时和 failed 状态，冻结指引内部不一致。；证据：E27（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:44–55）、E28（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md:17–30）、E12（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/README.zh-CN.md:36–40）、E66（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14）
- 实际行为证据：auth-provider 实际用十秒计时器把未完成的实时准备 publish 为 failed，并把 preparation/retryPreparation 放进 context；这是公开运行行为，不是仅文档声明。；证据：E13（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:83–105）、E66（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14）
- 应用接入与业务边界：verify-2 报告显示管理员登录成功且 authenticated=true，但进入业务界面即停在 Unable to connect，B01-B05 未执行；补丁显示应用原先把非 ready 渲染成 retry/sign-out 致命屏，后改为 failed 显示 warning 并继续挂载。；证据：E15（artifacts/verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–18）、E17（artifacts/agent.patch:1245–1304）、E14（app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:8–47）
- 环境因素：retro 记录原故障还包含 realtimeURL 从 api.baseURL 派生导致 Origin 不匹配 403，以及代理阻断 WebSocket；这解释了触发条件，但不能解释 Skill/参考为何漏列 preparation，因此环境因素不推翻文档缺口。；证据：E67（artifacts/retro.json:15–18）、E13（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:83–105）
- 工厂因素：补丁修改的是应用自有 .agents/rules/shell.md 和 session-preparation.tsx，致命门禁来自应用规则与实现；现有材料未发现工厂提示或注入配置直接要求该门禁，工厂因素保留未知，但不需要它来解释 Skill 缺项。；证据：E17（artifacts/agent.patch:1245–1304）、E14（app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:8–47）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：插件已公开 preparation/retryPreparation，README 也文档化其超时与 failed；当前应用已能用 failed+warning 降级。能力存在，缺的是 Skill/参考的入门说明，故落在 guidance-gap 而非 capability-gap。；证据：E12（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/README.zh-CN.md:36–40）、E14（app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:8–47）、E66（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

认证 Skill 的 Public surfaces 明确写 useAuthentication() 返回 client、session、isPending、refresh，client-session 参考也只描述这些字段；但实现实际还返回 preparation 和 retryPreparation，README 另有一段十秒准备超时与 retry/sign-out。Agent 使用了 preparation 并把 failed 实现成致命门禁，Skill 和参考都没有说明实时传输降级时是否应阻断 HTTP 会话。verify-2 因此登录成功后始终停在 Unable to connect，B01-B05 全部未执行。

**文档 / API 声称**：Skill 第 48 行称 useAuthentication() 返回 `{ client, session, isPending, refresh }`；client-session 参考只解释这些字段，并称 provider 不轮询、不监听撤销。

**实际观察**：auth-provider.js 实际暴露 preparation/retryPreparation，并在十秒后发布 failed；README 第 38-40 行也描述该状态和模板的 retry/sign-out 行为。

**影响**：按 Skill 的主路径接入时，应用无法从文档得知 preparation 的存在或正确的降级语义；一旦 WebSocket 握手失败，已认证业务界面可能完全不可达。

**建议改动**：把 preparation、retryPreparation、十秒超时和传输降级策略补进 Skill 与 client-session 参考，并给出“HTTP 健康但 socket 被阻断时继续挂载并显示 warning”的模板示例；说明认证失败与实时失败的区分。

**验收标准**：在阻断 WebSocket、保留 HTTP API 的环境中按 Skill 示例接入：业务路由可达并显示 warning；认证失效时仍回登录页；恢复 socket 后权限变更恢复实时生效。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：

- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md:17–30` — Client session 参考只描述 session/isPending/refresh，并说明 provider 不轮询、不监听撤销；没有说明首次实时准备的失败状态。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/README.zh-CN.md:36–40` — 认证文档说明 preparation 的 preparing/ready/failed、十秒初始化超时，以及默认模板在失败时只提供 retry 和 sign-out。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:83–105` — 客户端 provider 用十秒计时器把未完成的实时准备发布为 failed，并把 preparation 状态暴露给应用。

轻微易用性改进上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F5 · preparation 可增加“实时降级但认证可用”的状态，避免应用把 failed 等同于不可进入

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

类型确认 preparation 只有 preparing/ready/failed，且同一 context 还公开 session/isPending/refresh；auth-provider 在十秒后发布 failed，README 也只说 failed 提供 retry/sign-out。应用当前只能自行把 failed 解释为 warning（session-preparation.tsx），因此增加 authenticated-degraded 状态或能力标志是有源码依据的可用性改进：保持三态兼容，减少每个应用重复推断。现有 session+preparation 组合已能区分“未认证/认证中/已认证但实时 failed”，所以这不是缺失能力，而是便利性/模板层面的改进。

  • 职责与公开约定：AuthenticationContextValue 把 preparation 精确限定为 'preparing'|'ready'|'failed'，没有 degraded/authenticated 标志；同一 context 同时公开 session/isPending/refresh，调用方可以组合判断。 E66
  • 实际行为证据：provider 的十秒计时器到期 publish('failed')，failed 后没有额外状态；重试依靠 retryPreparation 新建握手，而不是切换到降级状态。 E13 E12
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用在 failed 时显示持久 warning 并继续挂载业务界面，说明非阻塞降级可做，但这段判断由应用重复实现；补丁只是把致命屏改成本地 warning+retry。 E14 E17
  • 环境因素：触发条件是 WebSocket 准备超时（代理阻断或 Origin 403），环境决定何时触发，不改变 hook 的状态集合；该改进基于类型/文档即可判断，不依赖运行时复现。 E13 E67
  • 工厂因素：插件 README 仍描述默认模板在 failed 时提供 retry/sign-out；应用侧规则和组件决定实际降级行为。现有材料未发现工厂提示要求新增状态，工厂是否应推动模板改动保留未知。 E12 E17
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：session 与 preparation 的正常组合已经能区分未认证/认证中/已认证但实时 failed；当前应用示例证明组合可用。命名 degraded 状态的价值是减少重复推断，属于改进空间而非缺失能力。 E66 E14

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

当前 useAuthentication 只暴露 preparing/ready/failed，认证插件内部同时掌握会话是否已认证和实时握手是否失败；应用只能自行判断 failed 是否致命。若公开状态能区分 authenticated-degraded，模板可直接采用非阻塞策略。

实际影响
可减少每个应用重复实现降级门禁，并降低把传输故障误判为登录故障的概率。
建议改法
在保持现有 preparation 兼容的前提下增加传输降级状态或能力标志，并在默认模板中使用；补充对应类型和示例。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
HTTP 会话已建立但 WebSocket 准备超时。
应有能力 / 行为
调用方能从公开 hook 区分“未认证/认证中”和“已认证但实时降级”。
实际观察
只有 failed，应用必须读取额外上下文或自行假设 HTTP 健康。
应用绕行与代价
应用检查 session 并直接把 failed 当 warning。
改进后的验收标准
类型和示例展示 degraded 状态；阻断 WebSocket 时业务界面可达，认证失效时仍回登录页。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/README.zh-CN.md:36–40E12

认证文档说明 preparation 的 preparing/ready/failed、十秒初始化超时，以及默认模板在失败时只提供 retry 和 sign-out。

### Client session preparation

`useAuthentication()` exposes `preparation` (`preparing`, `ready`, or `failed`) and `retryPreparation()`, separately from the session query's `isPending` and `refresh()`. For every new user/session pair, including cookie startup, the provider reconnects an already-created realtime client with active subscriptions. It publishes readiness after the synchronous open notification finishes, so authorization invalidation precedes the first page permission check. No realtime client or no subscriptions means immediate readiness without creating a connection. Initialization times out after ten seconds; a late open cannot release the failed attempt. Retry creates a fresh handshake, and logout or a new session cancels the old attempt. Superseded session queries cannot restore an old session.

The Default and Examples templates place `SessionPreparation` inside `RequiredAuthentication` and outside the application layout. The whole signed-in region waits automatically, including routes that skip page authorization; guest and optional routes keep their existing behavior. Failed initialization offers retry and sign-out, including feedback when sign-out fails. Custom applications using only `RequiredAuthentication` must explicitly consume preparation if they need this first-page guarantee. Runtime reconnects and permission notifications continue to invalidate authorization and unmount protected pages and their portals; preparation does not retain hidden content or replace server authorization. A custom application that starts subscriptions only after mounting pages remains subject to that runtime revalidation.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:83–105E13

客户端 provider 用十秒计时器把未完成的实时准备发布为 failed，并把 preparation 状态暴露给应用。

                publish('ready');
            });
        });
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            cancelled = true;
            unsubscribe();
            publish('failed');
        }, PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS);
        // Even cookie startup reconnects: connected alone does not prove identity.
        realtime.reconnect();
        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
            clearTimeout(timer);
            unsubscribe();
        };
    }, [isPending, sessionKey, signedIn, services, retry]);
    const preparation = !signedIn
        ? 'ready'
        : !isPending &&
            prepared?.sessionKey === sessionKey &&
            prepared.retry === retry
            ? prepared.status
            : 'preparing';
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:44–55E27

认证 Skill 的 Public surfaces 只声明 useAuthentication 返回 client/session/isPending/refresh，没有列出 preparation 或 retryPreparation。

Client, from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/client`:

- `authentication()` is the registration factory. It takes no options,
  contributes no routes, and mounts one React provider.
- `useAuthentication()` returns `{ client, session, isPending, refresh }`;
  `useAuthenticationClient()` returns the Better Auth client alone.
- `RequiredAuthentication`, `GuestAuthentication`, and
  `AuthenticationGuard({ mode })` gate a route subtree on the session.
- `AuthConfig` is Better Auth's client options, the type of the application's
  `client/config/auth.ts`.
- `authenticationClientToken` is the client's service token, used to stub the
  client in component tests.

### [轻微][易用性改进] preparation 可增加“实时降级但认证可用”的状态，避免应用把 failed 等同于不可进入

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.10）、`packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

类型确认 preparation 只有 preparing/ready/failed，且同一 context 还公开 session/isPending/refresh；auth-provider 在十秒后发布 failed，README 也只说 failed 提供 retry/sign-out。应用当前只能自行把 failed 解释为 warning（session-preparation.tsx），因此增加 authenticated-degraded 状态或能力标志是有源码依据的可用性改进：保持三态兼容，减少每个应用重复推断。现有 session+preparation 组合已能区分“未认证/认证中/已认证但实时 failed”，所以这不是缺失能力，而是便利性/模板层面的改进。

F2 · 应用修复绕过了致命门禁，但没有证明原始 WebSocket 握手故障已消除

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

补丁把 failed 状态改成 warning 并继续挂载，同时把 realtimeURL 固定为同源 /ws；但 retro 和 verify-3 没有记录原始握手的失败原因，也没有在 verify-3 中探测 /ws 或 realtime。verify-3 的 B01-B05 通过只能证明业务在降级模式下可用。

实际影响
若真实部署仍无法建立 WebSocket，权限变更通知和实时失效会持续缺失；当前通过不能证明服务端实时链路已恢复。
建议改法
在验收中加入 WebSocket 建连、权限变更推送和断线重连断言，并记录浏览器网络事件或服务端握手日志；在此之前不要把本项标记为已修复。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
原始环境中的 /ws 握手失败；具体代理、Origin、路径或服务端原因未记录。
应有能力 / 行为
修复应定位并消除握手失败，或明确声明采用降级模式且不影响业务。
实际观察
应用只改变失败后的 UI 行为；verify-3 未检查 realtime，因此根因仍未确认。
应用绕行与代价
保留 warning 并继续挂载，HTTP 业务可用。
改进后的验收标准
在原始失败环境重放并捕获 WebSocket upgrade 结果；建立连接后权限变更应实时生效，断开后重连成功。
E14 E15 E16 E17
实现者过程与运行背景 · 3 个过程问题 / 3 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

Restored browser usability after a fatal realtime preparation gate and delivered the requested library starting content. The application now mounts with a warning when the WebSocket cannot open, keeps realtime same-origin, and seeds the three sample documents and two business accounts through an opt-out sample seed.

问题 01 · verification

Application-owned LibraryProvider required the authorization plugin service

本任务已处理

tests/logic/app-server.test.ts > 'registers application-owned providers and routes from the runtime' failed with 'Service "@nocobase/app/authorization" is not registered.' at server/providers/library.ts:139, even though the test composes a runtime with an empty plugin list.

原因（实现者自述）
LibraryProvider.boot() resolved authorizationToken unconditionally. Application service providers are registered independently of installed plugins, so a runtime composed without app-plugin-authorization (as the test deliberately does) has no authorization service and boot aborted before the application-owned test provider ran.
处理方式
LibraryProvider.boot() now checks container.has(authorizationToken) and returns early when the authorization plugin is absent, with a comment explaining that provider boot must tolerate plugin-less runtime composition. Library permission declarations still register whenever the plugin is present, and the library routes continue to require it.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · verification

Fatal realtime preparation gate prevented every signed-in surface from loading

本任务已处理

After a successful admin sign-in, /main could show 'The realtime connection could not be prepared. Retry to enter the application, or sign out.' Retry, refresh, direct routes, and sign-out could all remain stuck, so no library page could be used.

原因（实现者自述）
SessionPreparation rendered a fatal retry/sign-out screen whenever the authentication provider's initial realtime reconnect timed out after ten seconds. A blocked or failed WebSocket therefore discarded an already valid HTTP session, even though route and action authorization is enforced server-side. The client also derived the realtime URL from api.baseURL, which can be absolute or cross-origin, while the realtime server rejects a mismatched Origin with 403.
处理方式
SessionPreparation now mounts the application with a persistent localized warning and a retry action when preparation times out. client/config/api.ts declares realtimeURL as the same-origin /ws path resolved from the application base. The raw handshake, Playwright, and agent-browser reproductions had not exposed the original failure, so this is a defensive repair; authorization remains server-side and fail-closed.
影响 / 代价：one repair cycle: source changes, focused component and runtime tests, typecheck, lint, and a production build来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 03 · verification

The requested sample documents and business accounts were absent from a fresh installation

本任务已处理

The browser check had no 甲 or 乙 account and no public, draft, or confidential sample document, so the library permissions could not be exercised as requested.

原因（实现者自述）
The repository had no sample data seed. A sample:true seed is skipped on a fresh factory-style install unless APP_SAMPLE_DATA is true, and the factory runtime configuration does not set it.
处理方式
Added database/main/seeds/202610200002_library_sample_records.ts with users 资料员甲 (jia) and 阅读者乙 (yi_reader), password library123, the library-maintainer and library-reader permission sets, and documents P, D, and C owned by 甲. server/config/app.ts now defaults app.sampleData to true for this application, while tests set APP_SAMPLE_DATA=false to keep their installations administrator-only. Verified the seed through a fresh db apply and a real browser sign-in.
影响 / 代价：one seed implementation plus a fresh-install browser verification来源：Agent · retro.json

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

框架职责与业务边界
框架提供共享规则的记录选择、subjects、action 前提和撤销语义；谁在何时共享哪条记录由业务 UI 决定。

E6 E22 E25

Agent 实际使用

E26

R01/R04 乙被停用后旧会话不能再访问，甲不受影响。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架负责账号停用事务、Permission Set 清理和每次会话解析时检查停用状态；业务只需调用管理 API。
推荐使用方式

Agent 实际使用

E10 E11 E16
停用操作不能留下仍可用的岗位授权。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架应在同一事务中断言可移除并移除 Permission Set subject。
推荐使用方式
使用 UserManagementService.disable，让角色 scope 与授权清理在同一事务完成。
Agent 实际使用
应用通过 Users 管理接口停用乙；verify-3 B05 观察到旧会话失效。

未记录能力缺口

需求满足度

92/100

Users 插件直接提供停用事务，认证在每次 getSession 重新检查 disabledAt，覆盖了本次“旧会话立即失效”的要求。

Agent 实际使用
原应用按默认门禁把 failed 当致命错误，verify-2 全应用不可达；修复后改为 warning 并继续挂载。

证据与原文

E1 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/README.md:91–101

README 规定每个请求创建一个授权上下文，并在该请求内复用；上下文提供 authorize/can/require 三种检查。

The principal is the authenticated actor; `subjects` adds verified memberships such as teams, and the principal itself also matches grants. `authz.for(identity)` uses the identity it is given: resolve memberships before calling it, or install identity middleware and use `authz.middleware()`, which resolves them per request.

| `AuthorizationContext` member | Result                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `identity`                    | The identity the context was created for.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `authorize(request)`          | The full decision: `permit`, `deny`, or `conditional` with `conditions`. For type `composite` the conditions are `CompositeResourceConditions`.                                                       |
| `can(request)`                | `true` only for `permit`. A conditional decision counts as `false`.                                                                                                                                   |
| `require(request)`            | Throws `AuthorizationDeniedError` unless the decision is `permit`. Uncaught in a Hono route, it answers `403` with the standard error body, reason `AUTHORIZATION_DENIED` and domain `authorization`. |
| `snapshot()`                  | `{ unrestricted, permissions }` for the client; see [Snapshot](#snapshot).                                                                                                                            |

Create one context per request and reuse it within that request only. Unknown types, conditional decisions without conditions and handler failures deny. Execute a conditional decision only through the adapter that understands its conditions.

原文件 SHA-256：a61ef5317620cbc7e09d0c485ba386f1b7d4c0223ab9c1bbd53965d6e0fbc93e

E2 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:82–99

授权实现按 identity 组合 grants 与 constraints，authorize 先检查 key scope，再进入统一的 grant 判定。

    for(identity) {
        const constraints = this.constraints.for(identity);
        const grants = composedGrants(this.provider.for?.(identity) ?? this.provider, this.compositeResourceRegistry, constraints);
        const request = (input) => ({
            ...input,
            principal: identity.principal,
            ...(identity.subjects === undefined
                ? {}
                : { subjects: identity.subjects }),
        });
        // A scoped credential is checked on the action requested only: the grants a composite action expands into are
        // the composite's business, so its internal checks run unscoped.
        const outOfScope = (input) => identity.keyScope &&
            !identity.keyScope.allows(input.resource, input.action)
            ? keyScopeDenial(identity.keyScope, input.resource, input.action)
            : undefined;
        const authorize = async (input) => outOfScope(input) ??
            (await this.authorizeWithGrants(request(input), grants, constraints));

原文件 SHA-256：9fa86eec3d540012300dfa6a2c3a20e19b0be2b0d6b0c5713ced9ab002ecdc9b

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/record-access.js:15–48

数据库插件内置 allRecords、recordsIOwn、recordsICreated、customFilter，并校验 record access 所需字段。

export const builtInRecordAccess = [
    {
        key: 'allRecords',
        collections: ['*'],
        title: title('options.recordAccessPolicies.allRecords'),
        resolve: () => true,
    },
    {
        key: 'recordsIOwn',
        collections: ['*'],
        requiredFields: ['ownerId'],
        title: title('options.recordAccessPolicies.recordsIOwn'),
        resolve: ({ principal, params }) => condition(params?.field ?? 'ownerId', '$eq', userId(principal)),
    },
    {
        key: 'recordsICreated',
        collections: ['*'],
        requiredFields: ['createdById'],
        title: title('options.recordAccessPolicies.recordsICreated'),
        resolve: ({ principal, params }) => condition(params?.field ?? 'createdById', '$eq', userId(principal)),
    },
    {
        key: 'customFilter',
        collections: ['*'],
        title: title('options.recordAccessPolicies.customFilter'),
        description: title('options.recordAccessPolicies.customFilterHint'),
        paramsSchema: { type: 'filter-node' },
        resolve: ({ params }) => {
            if (!params || typeof params !== 'object' || !('filter' in params))
                throw new Error('Custom Filter requires filter params');
            return params.filter;
        },
    },
];

原文件 SHA-256：15feef2694fdf265c3bddeba06d221e4c08fbf7045d700c5acfd1fd9d319d20f

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/authorization.js:15–60

createAppAuthorization 装配 permission sets、database、pages、settings、ui 插件，并注册请求身份中间件和 grants 变更通知。

export function createAppAuthorization(options) {
    const sets = options.config?.permissionSets;
    const connection = new DatabaseConnectionHandle('Permission Sets', options.connection);
    const authz = createAuthorization({
        connection: options.connection,
        onInvalidGrant: (grant) => options.onInvalidGrant?.(grant),
        plugins: [
            permissionSetsPlugin({
                store: new DatabasePermissionSetStore(connection.resolve),
                rootSet: {
                    key: sets?.rootSet ?? DEFAULT_ROOT_SET,
                    // `user` is this host's principal type: a superuser is an account.
                    assignableTo: ['user'],
                },
                defaultSet: sets?.defaultSet ?? DEFAULT_DEFAULT_SET,
            }),
            databasePlugin(options.database),
            pagesPlugin(),
            settingsPlugin(),
            uiPlugin(),
            ...(options.config?.plugins ?? []),
        ],
    });
    authz.subjects.add('authenticated', {
        filterActive: async (ids) => ids.filter((id) => id === '*'),
        administration: {
            title: text('options.subjectTypes.authenticated'),
            selection: { type: 'fixed', id: '*' },
        },
    });
    authz.use(async (request, next) => {
        const userId = readSessionUserId(request.http.var.auth);
        request.principal = { type: 'user', id: userId };
        request.subjects.add({ type: 'authenticated', id: '*' });
        for (const subject of await authz.subjects.resolveFor(request.principal))
            request.subjects.add(subject);
        await next();
    });
    authz.onGrantsChanged(async (subject) => {
        if (subject.type === 'user')
            await options.onUserPermissionsChanged?.(subject.id);
        // Any other subject reaches users through membership, which only its
        // owner can enumerate, so every client refreshes.
        else
            await options.onAuthenticatedPermissionsChanged?.();
    });

              f.boolean('published').isTrue(),
              f.boolean('confidential').isFalse(),

            const shared = f.and([
              f.boolean('confidential').isFalse(),
              f.or(sharedIds.map((id) => f.string('id').eq(id))),
            ]);
            return f.or([published, shared]);
          });

  public async updateDocument(
    documentId: string,
    input: UpdateDocumentInput,
  ): Promise<LibraryDocumentView> {
    const policy = await compositePolicy(this.authorization, 'edit');
    const values: Record<string, unknown> = { updatedAt: new Date() };
    if (input.title !== undefined) values.title = input.title;
    if (input.body !== undefined) values.body = input.body;
    if (input.published !== undefined) values.published = input.published;
    if (input.confidential !== undefined) {
      values.confidential = input.confidential;
    }
    await this.database
      .repository<LibraryDocumentRecord>('documents')
      .withPolicy(policy)
      .updateOne({
        filter: { id: documentId },
        values,
      });
    return this.getDocument(documentId);

E9 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:55–62

授权 Skill 给出声明数据库权限、组合 composite、注册 record access、服务端 withPolicy 和配置种子/权限集的明确顺序。

## Implement in this order

1. Declare schema and relationships through self-contained migrations. Choose stable composite, page and data scope names. Keep authorization decisions out of migration code.
2. Declare reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission`; compose user-facing business operations as composite resources with `defineCompositeResource` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`. Explicitly name fields, relation capabilities and every collection a workflow touches.
3. In the owning provider's `boot`, opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add`, register composites with `authz.compositeResources.define`, record access with `authz.recordAccess.define` and settings items with `authz.settings.add`. Then add a workspace subsection with `authz.ui.sections.add({ name, title, parent })` and list each composite or settings item in it with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section, group? })` (the permission workspace lists sections and subsections on the left and the selected subsection's resources, under optional `authz.ui.groups`, on the right; an unplaced composite or settings item goes under its default section's Other with a startup warning, and a misplaced one fails a development start). Pages are not registered on the server: the client route tree lists them. Release subject registrations at shutdown.
4. Protect every server route with authentication. For a simple single-scope Repository operation, keep `defineRepositoryApiRoutes` and add `authz.database.authorizeRepository` to map methods to composite actions; it reuses or initializes the request's authorization context. For workflows, persisted-state checks, multiple data scopes or custom responses, install `authz.middleware()`, call `c.get('authz').authorize()` once and bind every returned collection policy with `repository.withPolicy()`. Follow [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md).
5. Declare `authz` on every entry client route (nested pages inherit it; an omitted one defaults to root-only or `'skip'` with a development warning), add `useCan` for action visibility and obtain per-record eligibility from the server when needed. Handle pending and error states without displaying stale access.
6. Complete the accompanying permission configuration from the responsibility matrix: sets, page and action grants, selected data scopes, supported rules and intended assignments. Use seeds for fresh installations and authorized provisioning for existing installations; editing a seed does not update a running App. Resolve optional capabilities through their installed Skills and preserve unrelated administrator edits.

    async disable(userId) {
        const user = await this.services.database.transaction(async (connection) => {
            for (const scope of this.services.roleScopes.list()) {
                await scope.assertCanDisable?.(userId, connection);
            }
            // A disabled account can no longer act, so disabling it removes the
            // subject from every Permission Set as surely as revoking would.
            await this.services.permissionSets
                ?.withTransaction(connection)
                .assertSubjectRemovable({ type: 'user', id: userId });
            await lockUserForAdministration(connection, userId);
            return this.services.users.withConnection(connection).disable(userId);
        });
        return this.withRoleScopes(user, this.services.database.connection());

E11 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:203–222

getSession 在返回会话前重新查询 user.disabledAt；用户不存在或已停用时返回 null，旧会话不能继续认证。

    async getSession(headers, options = {}) {
        for (const resolve of [...this.credentialResolvers]) {
            const credential = await resolve(headers);
            if (credential)
                return this.credentialSession(credential);
        }
        const session = await this.auth.api.getSession({
            headers,
            ...(options.disableRefresh ? { query: { disableRefresh: true } } : {}),
        });
        if (!session)
            return null;
        const user = await this.connection.query
            .selectFrom('user')
            .select(['id', 'disabledAt'])
            .where('id', '=', session.user.id)
            .executeTakeFirst();
        if (!user || user.disabledAt != null)
            return null;
        return session;

E12 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/README.zh-CN.md:36–40

认证文档说明 preparation 的 preparing/ready/failed、十秒初始化超时，以及默认模板在失败时只提供 retry 和 sign-out。

### Client session preparation

`useAuthentication()` exposes `preparation` (`preparing`, `ready`, or `failed`) and `retryPreparation()`, separately from the session query's `isPending` and `refresh()`. For every new user/session pair, including cookie startup, the provider reconnects an already-created realtime client with active subscriptions. It publishes readiness after the synchronous open notification finishes, so authorization invalidation precedes the first page permission check. No realtime client or no subscriptions means immediate readiness without creating a connection. Initialization times out after ten seconds; a late open cannot release the failed attempt. Retry creates a fresh handshake, and logout or a new session cancels the old attempt. Superseded session queries cannot restore an old session.

The Default and Examples templates place `SessionPreparation` inside `RequiredAuthentication` and outside the application layout. The whole signed-in region waits automatically, including routes that skip page authorization; guest and optional routes keep their existing behavior. Failed initialization offers retry and sign-out, including feedback when sign-out fails. Custom applications using only `RequiredAuthentication` must explicitly consume preparation if they need this first-page guarantee. Runtime reconnects and permission notifications continue to invalidate authorization and unmount protected pages and their portals; preparation does not retain hidden content or replace server authorization. A custom application that starts subscriptions only after mounting pages remains subject to that runtime revalidation.

            });
        });
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            cancelled = true;
            unsubscribe();
            publish('failed');
        }, PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS);
        // Even cookie startup reconnects: connected alone does not prove identity.
        realtime.reconnect();

            cancelled = true;
            clearTimeout(timer);
            unsubscribe();

    }, [isPending, sessionKey, signedIn, services, retry]);
    const preparation = !signedIn
        ? 'ready'
        : !isPending &&
            prepared?.sessionKey === sessionKey &&
            prepared.retry === retry
            ? prepared.status
            : 'preparing';

E14 · app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:8–47

当前应用把 failed 准备降级为持久 warning 并继续挂载业务界面，不再让 HTTP 可用的会话停在致命屏。

/**
 * Waits for the first identity handshake, so pages mount with a permission snapshot for the signed-in session.
 *
 * A realtime connection can be unavailable while the HTTP API and the session are healthy: a proxy may block the
 * WebSocket, or the server may be briefly unable to resolve the session when the socket opens. The authorization
 * revision still guards every route and invalidates permissions on later notifications, so an unavailable socket must
 * not lock the user out of an otherwise working application. The handshake therefore falls back to the mounted
 * application with a warning, instead of a fatal screen that only offers retry or sign-out.
 */
export function SessionPreparation({
  children,
}: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement {
  const { preparation, retryPreparation } = useAuthentication();
  const toaster = useToaster();
  const { t } = useTranslation();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (preparation !== 'failed') return;
    const id = 'session-preparation-realtime-unavailable';
    toaster.show({
      id,
      type: 'warning',
      title: t('auth.realtimeUnavailable'),
      description: t('auth.realtimeUnavailableDescription'),
      duration: 0,
      action: {
        label: t('auth.retry'),
        onClick: () => {
          toaster.close(id);
          retryPreparation();
        },
      },
    });
  }, [preparation, retryPreparation, t, toaster]);

  if (preparation === 'preparing') {
    return <Loading className='min-h-svh' label={t('auth.preparingSession')} />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;

原文件 SHA-256：65368334e8121de3646abdac2c03965c65a9cc9d71e358bd6e0b108a8d166e5d

E18 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/dist/server/authorization.js:1–31

sharingRules 工厂注册共享规则 store、设置项、UI 位置和 /authorization/sharingRules 路由。

import { sharingRulesPlugin, } from '@nocobase/authorization/sharing-rules';
import { AUTHORIZATION_SETTINGS_SECTION, } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server';
import { DatabaseConnectionHandle } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server/extension';
import { createSharingRulesHandler, SHARING_RULES_PATH, SHARING_RULES_SETTINGS, } from './handler.js';
import { DatabaseSharingRuleStore } from './stores/sharing-rules.js';
const NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules';
const APP_NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization';
/** The configuration factory: sharing rules with their settings item and routes. */
export function sharingRules(options = {}) {
    const connection = new DatabaseConnectionHandle('Sharing Rules');
    const plugin = sharingRulesPlugin({
        store: options.store ?? new DatabaseSharingRuleStore(connection.resolve),
    });
    return {
        ...plugin,
        dependencies: ['settings', 'database', 'ui'],
        setup(authz) {
            connection.set(authz.connection);
            plugin.setup?.(authz);
            authz.settings.add({
                id: SHARING_RULES_SETTINGS,
                title: { key: 'resourceTitle', ns: NAMESPACE },
                actions: ['read', 'create', 'update', 'delete'].map((name) => ({
                    name,
                    title: { key: `options.actions.${name}`, ns: APP_NAMESPACE },
                })),
            });
            authz.ui.place({ type: 'settings', id: SHARING_RULES_SETTINGS }, { section: AUTHORIZATION_SETTINGS_SECTION, order: 20 });
            authz.routes.add(SHARING_RULES_PATH, createSharingRulesHandler(authz, plugin.authorizationApi.sharingRules));
        },
    };

E22 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:8–21

共享 Skill 明确共享只对已有 action 的 subjects 增加所选记录，不授予 operation/page/fields，撤销时只移除该来源。

A sharing rule adds selected records, or a record access selection, for the subjects it lists, provided they already hold the action. Sharing does not grant the operation, fields, page access or related records, and it cannot select all records. Grants, default access and sharing combine first; restriction rules then narrow the result.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For sharing between departments, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. A rule whose recipient is a department shares specific records with it; a viewer-relative department scope such as 本部门及下属部门 is computed for the viewer and cannot select another department's records. The guide's optional sharing section covers this plugin, and its core section says what remains with permission sets alone.

## Development workflow

1. Identify the real collaboration exception: who receives which records, for which actions, and why. Use explicit records for one handover; use a record access selection for a maintained region or team rule.
2. Grant the recipients the business action through a permission set first. Register inherited subjects and their active membership resolver through the main authorization Skill.
3. Inspect every data scope the operation requires. Quote submission needs the selected quote and its actual parent project. Add each action and data scope deliberately; view, edit and submit are different permissions.
4. Save a complete rule through the HTTP routes or the service. Keep record ids tied to their data scope's collection and preserve the other entries on update.
5. Verify the handover works, an unshared parent still blocks the workflow, restrictions still exclude confidential records, and sharing alone cannot activate a missing operation.
6. Revoke the recipient or the team's permission set and verify the next request loses only that source. Independent direct-user sharing and direct assignments must survive.

原文件 SHA-256：dc2fa805a49ad3a753e5c9005eb404afbed7d5ec0a1da14b0642dc51a4f80ba8

E23 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules/SKILL.md:8–20

限制 Skill 明确规则与记录范围取交集、不能授予 action，并要求对每个受保护读写绑定策略后再验证排除记录。

A restriction rule intersects the records an action reaches with the rule's selection, for the subjects it lists. The selection describes the records still allowed, not the records to hide. Several matching restrictions all apply; none can grant an action or widen a range.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For a restriction assigned to a department, or to the root department for the whole company, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. Its core works with permission sets alone and says how to keep excluded records out without this plugin; its optional restriction section covers this one.

## Development workflow

1. State the invariant positively, for example "this team may access only non-confidential projects". Define who it applies to and whether it covers one operation or every path to a collection.
2. Use a rule on a composite, with `scopeKey`, for an operation-specific limit. For a limit across every business branch, create a rule on the `database.collection` with its CRUD actions; declared business data scopes and collection-wide invariants are both provisioned through the HTTP routes or the service.
3. Register a suitable record access and save the complete rule. Apply the restriction directly to a user as well when it must remain after that person's team membership is removed.
4. Bind policies on all protected reads and writes. For relation targets, declare relation record access explicitly; a standalone collection restriction is not inherited by nested relation writes.
5. Verify the excluded row stays inaccessible after adding broad sharing and another permission set, across each protected operation, and that unrelated actors keep their intended access.

function registerReadableAccess(
  authz: AppAuthorization,
  database: DatabaseManager,
): void {
  authz.recordAccess.define(
    defineRecordAccess(LIBRARY_VISIBLE_ACCESS_KEY, (access) =>
      access
        .title({ key: 'library.permissions.visible', ns: 'nb3-factory' })
        .description({
          key: 'library.permissions.visibleDescription',
          ns: 'nb3-factory',
        })
        .collections('documents')
        .resolver(async ({ principal }) => {
          if (principal.type !== 'user') return false;
          return buildFilter((f) =>
            f.or([
              f.string('ownerId').eq(principal.id),
              f.and([
                f.boolean('published').isTrue(),
                f.boolean('confidential').isFalse(),
              ]),
            ]),
          );
        }),
    ),
  );

E25 · app/server/library/service.ts:381–432

createShare 校验目标存在、是启用的人员账号，并拒绝共享保密资料；共享由应用自己的 documentShares 记录维护。

  public async createShare(
    documentId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<LibraryShareView> {
    const [document, user, existing] = await Promise.all([
      this.database
        .query()
        .selectFrom('documents')
        .select(['id', 'confidential'])
        .where('id', '=', documentId)
        .executeTakeFirst(),
      this.database
        .query()
        .selectFrom('user')
        .select(['id', 'name', 'email', 'kind', 'disabledAt', 'deletedAt'])
        .where('id', '=', userId)
        .executeTakeFirst(),
      this.database
        .query()
        .selectFrom('documentShares')
        .select('id')
        .where('documentId', '=', documentId)
        .where('userId', '=', userId)
        .executeTakeFirst(),
    ]);
    if (!document) throw notFound(documentId);
    if (
      !user ||
      user.kind !== 'person' ||
      user.disabledAt !== null ||
      user.deletedAt !== null
    ) {
      throw new ApiError({
        status: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
        reason: 'LIBRARY_SHARE_USER_INVALID',
        domain: 'library',
        message: 'A share needs an enabled person account.',
        fieldViolations: [
          {
            field: 'userId',
            description: 'The user is not an enabled person.',
          },
        ],
      });
    }
    if (document.confidential) {
      throw new ApiError({
        status: 'FAILED_PRECONDITION',
        reason: 'LIBRARY_DOCUMENT_CONFIDENTIAL',
        domain: 'library',
        message: 'A confidential document cannot be shared.',
      });

E26 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-users/SKILL.md:22–35

Users Skill 列出受认证与授权保护的 disable/revokeSessions 等 HTTP API，并要求应用用 UsersClient 构建管理页。

- Server contracts: `userManagementServiceToken`,
  `userRoleScopeRegistryToken`, `UserRoleScope`, and related types from
  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/server/tokens`.
- HTTP API: `GET /api/users` (`page`, `pageSize`, `q`, `status`, `roleScope`, `role`, `sort=name`, `order=asc|desc`; answers `{ data, meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`), `GET /api/users/options`, `GET /api/users/:userId` (latest managed profile and role scopes, requires `user/read`, 404 for absent accounts), `POST /api/users`, `PATCH /api/users/:userId`, `DELETE /api/users/:userId?confirm=true`, `POST /api/users/:userId/disable|enable|resetPassword|revokeSessions`, and `PUT /api/users/:userId/roleScopes/:scope`. The running application documents each one, with request and response schemas, at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`) under the `Users` tag. Failures use the standard error body; branch on `error.reason`, such as `USER_NOT_FOUND`, `SELF_DELETE_NOT_ALLOWED`, `LAST_ASSIGNMENT` or `USER_EMAIL_CONFLICT`.

Every HTTP route requires Authentication and Authorization. Routes check the `user` record type with one of `read`, `create`, `update`, `disable`, `enable`, `assign-role`, `reset-password`, `revoke-sessions` or `delete`. Account creation checks both `create` and `assign-role`. `delete` is permitted only while an application role scope can clean a deleted user up.

## Register the plugin

1. Register Authentication, Authorization, and then Users in the App's Client
   and Server plugin arrays. The Client factory takes no placement options:
   `users()`.
2. Grant only the `user` actions those roles need, on `{ type: 'user', id: '*' }` because `user` is a record type. The plugin creates no roles and grants no access by itself.
3. For a user management page, build it in the App on `UsersClient` and declare it among the App's own routes with the page access it needs; the plugin ships none.

E27 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:44–55

认证 Skill 的 Public surfaces 只声明 useAuthentication 返回 client/session/isPending/refresh，没有列出 preparation 或 retryPreparation。

Client, from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/client`:

- `authentication()` is the registration factory. It takes no options,
  contributes no routes, and mounts one React provider.
- `useAuthentication()` returns `{ client, session, isPending, refresh }`;
  `useAuthenticationClient()` returns the Better Auth client alone.
- `RequiredAuthentication`, `GuestAuthentication`, and
  `AuthenticationGuard({ mode })` gate a route subtree on the session.
- `AuthConfig` is Better Auth's client options, the type of the application's
  `client/config/auth.ts`.
- `authenticationClientToken` is the client's service token, used to stub the
  client in component tests.

原文件 SHA-256：a9620d8f9c4fc67f4b98f791ca5e1b1d2ad4b629f21d014fd02e411275f17b36

E28 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md:17–30

Client session 参考只描述 session/isPending/refresh，并说明 provider 不轮询、不监听撤销；没有说明首次实时准备的失败状态。

- `session` is `null` while anonymous; `session.user` has `id`, `name`,
  `email`, `username`, `image`, and `emailVerified`.
- `isPending` is true until the first `getSession()` completes and during any
  `refresh()`. Render nothing or a placeholder while pending; do not treat
  pending as anonymous.
- `refresh()` re-reads the session from the server. Call it after any action
  that changes the session outside the provided hooks.
- `client` is the Better Auth client. Every Better Auth client plugin
  configured in `client/config/auth.ts` is available on it.

The provider does not poll and does not listen for revocation. A session
revoked on the server is noticed at the next `refresh()` or when an API call
returns `401`; treat that `401` as "sign in again", not as an application
error.

A toast shown earlier than that, from a component mounted\n * together with the `Toaster`, would be dropped.\n */\nexport function createToaster(manager: typeof toast = toast): Toaster {\n  return {\n    show: (options: ToastOptions): string =>\n      manager.add({\n        id: options.id,\n        type: options.type,\n        title: options.title,\n        description: options.description,\n        timeout: options.duration,\n        priority: isUrgent(options) ? E66 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14

公开类型 AuthenticationContextValue 把 preparation 限定为 'preparing' | 'ready' | 'failed'，同时暴露 client/session/isPending/refresh/retryPreparation；没有 authenticated-degraded 或 transport 降级标志。

export interface AuthenticationContextValue {
    readonly client: AuthClient;
    readonly session: AuthClient['$Infer']['Session'] | null;
    readonly isPending: boolean;
    readonly refresh: () => Promise<void>;
    /** First realtime identity preparation; independent of the session query. */
    readonly preparation: 'preparing' | 'ready' | 'failed';
    readonly retryPreparation: () => void;
}
export declare function AuthenticationProvider({ children, }: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement;
export declare function useAuthentication(): AuthenticationContextValue;
export declare function useAuthenticationClient(): AuthClient;

E67 · artifacts/retro.json:15–18

retro 记录 fatal realtime preparation gate 的 rootCause：SessionPreparation 在十秒超时后渲染致命 retry/sign-out 屏，且 realtimeURL 从 api.baseURL 派生会被 realtime server 以 Origin 不匹配 403 拒绝；resolution 改为 warning+retry 并使用同源 /ws，且称原始 handshake/Playwright/agent-browser 复现未暴露该故障。

      "title": "Fatal realtime preparation gate prevented every signed-in surface from loading",
      "symptom": "After a successful admin sign-in, /main could show 'The realtime connection could not be prepared. Retry to enter the application, or sign out.' Retry, refresh, direct routes, and sign-out could all remain stuck, so no library page could be used.",
      "rootCause": "SessionPreparation rendered a fatal retry/sign-out screen whenever the authentication provider's initial realtime reconnect timed out after ten seconds. A blocked or failed WebSocket therefore discarded an already valid HTTP session, even though route and action authorization is enforced server-side. The client also derived the realtime URL from api.baseURL, which can be absolute or cross-origin, while the realtime server rejects a mismatched Origin with 403.",
      "resolution": "SessionPreparation now mounts the application with a persistent localized warning and a retry action when preparation times out. client/config/api.ts declares realtimeURL as the same-origin /ws path resolved from the application base. The raw handshake, Playwright, and agent-browser reproductions had not exposed the original failure, so this is a defensive repair; authorization remains server-side and fail-closed.",

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

按人按份临时共享

管理员可把单份草稿 D 临时开放给阅读者乙；乙只获得该份资料的阅读能力，未获得编辑权或其他资料访问权。

撤销共享立即生效

撤销 D 后，乙刷新详情或重新打开原链接均只看到不可用提示，正文不再出现。

保密资料受保护

系统明确拒绝把保密资料 C 开放给阅读者；乙直接打开 C 也无法看到正文，资料员甲的读取和维护不受影响。

停用账号终止旧会话

管理员停用乙后，乙原有会话刷新即回到登录页；甲仍能正常读取 C 和维护自己的资料。

01管理员登录并查看资料库
→
02资料员甲编辑草稿 D 并验证原值回填
→
03阅读者乙阅读公开资料 P，直接打开 D 被拒绝
→
04管理员将草稿 D 临时开放给乙
→
05乙查看 D，确认无编辑权限且列表中没有 C
→
06管理员撤销 D，乙刷新和重开均无法看到正文
→
07管理员尝试开放保密 C 被拒绝，甲仍可读取 C
→
08管理员停用乙，乙旧会话回到登录页，甲访问不受影响

业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

验收记录

每项直接显示结果；展开可看原始要求、操作记录、观察结果和截图。证据批注单独显示，不改写原始验收状态。

01

乙可以阅读 P 但不能编辑；甲可以维护自己的资料。

乙身份未进入任何编辑表单；甲可正常打开、编辑并保存自己负责的 D 和 P。

A1 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

乙可以阅读 P 但不能编辑；甲可以维护自己的资料。

实际操作

  1. 阅读者乙登录后从资料库打开公开资料 P，查看正文和可用操作。
  2. 资料员甲登录后打开私有草稿 D，点击 Edit 检查标题与正文原值回填，修改正文并保存。
  3. 甲重新打开 D 的编辑弹窗，将正文恢复为原值并再次保存，确认原值回填及可维护性。

观察结果

  1. 乙的 P 详情页显示完整正文，页面没有 Edit、Delete 或其他编辑入口。
  2. 甲的 D 编辑弹窗回填标题“私有草稿 D”和原正文；保存后详情更新时间变化并显示新增内容；恢复原正文后再次保存成功。
  3. 乙身份未进入任何编辑表单；甲可正常打开、编辑并保存自己负责的 D 和 P。

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

02

乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

共享后乙打开 D 能看到“这是资料员甲的未发布草稿……”正文，但详情没有 Edit/Delete；资料库仅列出 D 和 P，保密资料 C 不在乙的列表中。

A2 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

实际操作

  1. 乙在共享前直接打开 D 的 URL，确认正文不可见。
  2. 管理员在 Document sharing 中选择 D 和阅读者乙，点击 Share 完成开放。
  3. 乙重新打开 D 的 URL，查看正文、可用操作及资料库列表中的其他资料。

观察结果

  1. 共享前乙打开 D 只看到资料库和“Document not available”，没有正文。
  2. 管理员页面提示“The draft is now shared.”，D 下显示阅读者乙及 Revoke。
  3. 共享后乙打开 D 能看到“这是资料员甲的未发布草稿……”正文，但详情没有 Edit/Delete；资料库仅列出 D 和 P，保密资料 C 不在乙的列表中。

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

03

撤销 D 的开放后，乙刷新详情或再次打开原链接，都看不到正文。

乙刷新 D 详情后同样显示“Document not available”，未恢复正文。

A3 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

撤销 D 的开放后，乙刷新详情或再次打开原链接，都看不到正文。

实际操作

  1. 管理员在 Document sharing 中点击 D 的 Revoke，确认开放记录消失。
  2. 乙打开 D 原链接，等待页面加载完成，检查正文。
  3. 乙停留在 D 详情页执行刷新，再次检查正文。

观察结果

  1. 管理员页面提示“Access was revoked.”，D 下显示“No one has temporary access to this document.”。
  2. 撤销后乙再次打开 D 原链接，页面显示“Document not available”，无正文，资料库只剩 P。
  3. 乙刷新 D 详情后同样显示“Document not available”，未恢复正文。

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

04

即使给乙开放了 C，乙仍看不到保密内容，甲仍可正常读取。

甲打开 C 显示完整保密正文及 Edit/Delete，资料库仍显示 P、D、C 三份资料。

A4 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

即使给乙开放了 C，乙仍看不到保密内容，甲仍可正常读取。

实际操作

  1. 管理员在 Document sharing 中选择保密资料 C 和阅读者乙，尝试点击 Share。
  2. 乙以原有阅读者会话直接打开 C 的 URL，检查正文和资料库列表。
  3. 甲以自己的资料员会话直接打开 C，检查正文和维护操作。

观察结果

  1. 选择 C 和乙后 Share 按钮保持不可用，点击后显示“This document is confidential and cannot be shared with a reader.”；共享记录仍为空，系统没有把 C 开放给乙。
  2. 乙直接打开 C 显示“Document not available”，没有保密正文，资料库仍只有 P。
  3. 甲打开 C 显示完整保密正文及 Edit/Delete，资料库仍显示 P、D、C 三份资料。

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

05

停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。

甲刷新后 C 详情仍显示完整保密正文及 Edit/Delete，资料库仍显示 P、D、C 三份资料；甲的访问未受影响。

A5 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。

实际操作

  1. 管理员在 Users 中对阅读者乙执行 Disable，并在确认框点击 Disable。
  2. 在乙停用前已登录的原有浏览器会话中刷新原标签页；agent-browser 对该会话的命令超时，同会话 close 也超时，随后通过该标签页已有的浏览器调试连接执行 Page.reload 完成真实刷新。
  3. 甲在自己的会话中完整刷新保密资料 C 的详情页，检查正文、维护操作和三份资料。

观察结果

  1. 管理员确认框说明该用户将无法登录且现有会话会结束；提交后 Users 列表显示乙为 Disabled，并提示“Updated access for 阅读者乙”。
  2. 乙原会话刷新后 URL 变为 http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/login，页面为登录表单，不再显示资料库或任何资料正文。
  3. 甲刷新后 C 详情仍显示完整保密正文及 Edit/Delete，资料库仍显示 P、D、C 三份资料；甲的访问未受影响。

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

完整原始验收要求
B01. 乙可以阅读 P 但不能编辑；甲可以维护自己的资料。

B02. 乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

B03. 撤销 D 的开放后，乙刷新详情或再次打开原链接，都看不到正文。

B04. 即使给乙开放了 C，乙仍看不到保密内容，甲仍可正常读取。

B05. 停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。
